# Verbs (Bulgarian)

Verbs in [Bulgarian](./Bulgarian.md) inflect for person, number, aspect, tense and mood. They have no infinitives. In dictionaries, verbs are listed by their first-person singular present simple form and their [conjugation](#Conjugation).

## Aspect

Every [verb](TODO) in [Bulgarian](./Bulgarian.md) has [aspect](TODO) (**вид**) and belongs to one of two categories - **imperfective aspect** (**несвършен вид**) and **perfrective aspect** (**свършен вид**). The vast majority of verbs must thus be learnt in aspectual pairs.

### Imperfective Verbs

Imperfective verbs represent actions which are in progress, habitual or repeatedly occuring.

Most [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) formed via the suffixes ⟨ув⟩, ⟨ов⟩ and ⟨в⟩ without a prefix are imperfective.

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Imperfective Verbs with Suffixes ⟨ув⟩, ⟨ов⟩ and ⟨в⟩
>
>|Example|Translation|Notes|
>|:--:|:--:|:--:|
>|аресту̀вам|to arrest||
>|атаку̀вам|to attack||
>|гладу̀вам|to be hungry||
>|госту̀вам|to be a guest||
>|дѐйствам|to act||
>|клюка̀рствам|to gossip||
>|критику̀вам|to criticize||
>|лову̀вам|to hunt||
>|нощу̀вам|to spend the night||
>|публику̀вам|to make sth public||
>

### Perfective Verbs

Perfective verbs глаголи от свършен вид represent actions which are viewed as a complete whole, without reference to duration or repetitiveness.

## Present Forms

The present first-person singular serves as the dictionary form of a [verb](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md). It is also the only present form which might be completely irregular.

Building the rest of the present forms requires the present stem which can be obtained in any one of the following ways:

- the present third-person singular, which is always identical to the present stem;
- by removing the ending *-ш* from the present second-person singular;
- by removing the ending *-те* from the present second-person plural.

Dictionaries usually list the present second-person singular to show the present stem.

Based on the ending of the present stem, [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) are divided into three groups:

- **first conjugation** with a present stem in *-е*;
- **second conjugation** with a present stem in *-и*;
- **third conjugation** with a present stem in *-а* or *-я*.

The endings which a [verb](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) takes in the present forms are determined by its conjugation group:

<table>
<caption>Present Form Endings</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" colspan=2></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Conjugation</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Conjugation</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Conjugation</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-а</i>, <i>-я</i>, <i>-м</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-я</i>, <i>-а</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-м</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" colspan=3><i>-ш</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" colspan=3>-</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Plural</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" colspan=2><i>-м</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ме</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" colspan=3><i>-те</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ат</i>, <i>-ят</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ят</i>, <i>-ат</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-т</i></td>
</tr>
</table>

When [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress), the endings *-а*, *-ат*, *-я* and *-ят* are pronounced [ɤ], [ɤt], [jɤ] and [jɤt], respectively.

Building the present forms is done by  attaching the appropriate ending to the present stem with a few additional rules depending on the [verb](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md)'s conjugation group. There are no [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) shifts, except for irregular first-person singular forms which do not use the entire present stem.

The [verb](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) *съм* ('to be') is the only one with completely irregular present forms:

<table>
<caption>съм ('to be')</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">съм<br>('I am')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">сме<br>('we are')</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">си<br>('you are')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">сте<br>('you are')</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">е<br>('he / she / it is')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">са<br>('they are')</td>
</tr>
</table>

### First-Conjugation Present Forms

[Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) of the first conjugation take the following endings in the present forms:

<table>
<caption>Present Endings (First Conjugation)</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-а</i>, <i>-я</i>, <i>-м</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-м</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ш</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-те</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ат</i>, <i>-ят</i></td>
</tr>
</table>

- The vast majority of first-conjugation verbs have a first-person singular ending in *-а*. The ending *-я* comes after ⟨а⟩, ⟨ъ⟩, ⟨о⟩, ⟨у⟩, ⟨е⟩, ⟨и⟩, ⟨ю⟩ and ⟨я⟩ as well in a few other verbs. A very small number of [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) use the ending *-м*.
- The thematic vowel is dropped in the first-person singular (when regular) and the third-person plural.
- Verbs with first-person singular in *-ка*, *-га* alternate ⟨к⟩, ⟨г⟩ with ⟨ч⟩, ⟨ж⟩ before the thematic vowel ⟨е⟩.
- One should beware changes in [variable ya](./Yat%20Reflex%20(Bulgarian).md).

><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>

><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐзем</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The variable ya is ⟨е⟩ in the forms in which it is followed by the thematic vowel ⟨е⟩.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐзеш</td>

></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>

><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>

><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ядѐм</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The first-person singular form <i>ям</i> is irregular. The endings <i>-а</i>, <i>-ат</i> are used instead of <i>-я</i>, <i>-ят</i> since they are not preceded by ⟨а⟩, ⟨ъ⟩, ⟨о⟩, ⟨у⟩, ⟨е⟩, ⟨и⟩, ⟨ю⟩ or ⟨я⟩.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ядѐш</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ядѐте</td>
></tr>

><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ядѐ</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">яда̀т</td>
></tr>
></table>
>

### Second-Conjugation Verbs

[Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) of the second conjugation take the following endings in the present forms:

<table>
<caption>Present Endings (Second Conjugation)</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-я</i>, <i>-а</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-м</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ш</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-те</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ят</i>, <i>-ат</i></td>
</tr>
</table>

- The endings *-а* (first-person singular) and *-ат* (third-person plural) are used only after ⟨ж⟩, ⟨ч⟩ or ⟨ш⟩.
- The thematic vowel is dropped in the first-person singular (when regular) and in the third-person plural.

></tr>

### Third-Conjugation Present Forms

[Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) of the third conjugation take the following endings in the present forms:

<table>
<caption>Present Endings (Third Conjugation)</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-м</i>,</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ме</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ш</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-те</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-т</i></td>
</tr>
</table>

An interesting phenomenon is that the present forms third-conjugation [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) are never [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the ending.

## Aorist Forms

Constructing the aorist forms required the aorist stem and thus listed separately in dictionaries. This is done by attaching the appropriate ending to the aorist stem:

<table>
<caption>Aorist Endings</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-х</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-хме</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-хте</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-</i></td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"><i>-ха</i></td>
</tr>
</table> 

The [verb](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) *съм* ('to be') is the only one with completely irregular present forms:

<table>
<caption>съм ('to be')</caption>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бя̀х, бидо̀х<br>('I was')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бя̀хме, бидо̀хме<br>('we were')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The forms <i>бидох</i>, <i>биде</i>, <i>бидохме</i>, <i>бидохте</i> and <i>бидоха</i> are almost never used nowadays. A similar tendency is taking shape with the form <i>бе</i></td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бе, бѐше, бидѐ<br>('you were')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бя̀хте, бидо̀хте<br>('you were')</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бе, бѐше, бидѐ<br>('he / she / it was')</td>
<td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бя̀ха, бидо̀ха<br>('they were')</td>
</tr>
</table>

Each [conjugation class](#Present%20Forms) is divided into **subclasses** (**разреди**) each of which with a specific [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) and potentially additional rules on how the [aorist](#Aorist%20Forms) forms are constructed.

### First-Conjugation Aorist Forms

The first subclass of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contains the [verbs](../Serbo-Croatian/Verbs.md) whose [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is derived from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) by replacing the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ with the aorist thematic vowel ⟨о⟩ / ⟨е⟩. Specifically, the aorist thematic vowel remains ⟨е⟩ in the second- and third-person singular and is ⟨о⟩ in the rest of the [aorist forms](#Aorist%20Forms). 

A few things to note:

- These are typically [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) whose [present](#Present%20Tense) first-person singular ends in *-да*, *-та*, *-за*, *-са* or *-ка* with an etymologically non-palatalized ⟨д⟩, ⟨т⟩, ⟨з⟩, ⟨с⟩, ⟨к⟩.
- In [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a [present](#Present%20Tense) first-person singular ending in *-ка*, the ⟨к⟩ alternates with ⟨ч⟩ before the aorist thematic vowel ⟨е⟩.
- If the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) is [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the present thematic vowel, then the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the syllable before the aorist thematic vowel.
- If the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) is not [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the present thematic vowel, then the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the same place as the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).
- One should beware changes in [variable ya](./Yat%20Reflex%20(Bulgarian).md).

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclass 1 of Conjugation Class I
>
><table>
><caption>бода̀, бодѐш, бо̀дох ('to sting', imperfective)</caption>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бо̀дох</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">бо̀дохме</td>
><td style="text-align:left;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The stress of the aorist stem falls on the syllable before the aorist thematic vowel since the stress of the present stem falls on the present thematic vowel.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>

><table>
><caption>вля̀за, влѐзеш, вля̀зох, влѐзе ('to enter', perfective)</caption>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀зох</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀зохме</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The stress of the aorist stem and the present stem is identical since the present stem is not stressed on the present thematic vowel. The variable ya is ⟨е⟩ in the second- and third-person singular since the next syllable contains ⟨е⟩.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐзе</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀зохте</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐзе</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀зоха</td>
></tr>
></table>
>
><table>
><caption>влека̀, влечѐш, вля̀кох, влѐче ('to drag', 'to pull', imperfective)</caption>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀кох</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀кохме</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>The ⟨к⟩ alternates with ⟨ч⟩ before the aorist thematic vowel ⟨е⟩. The stress of the aorist stem falls on the syllable before the aorist thematic vowel since the stress of the present stem falls on the present thematic vowel. The variable ya is ⟨е⟩ in the second- and third-person singular since the next syllable contains ⟨е⟩.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐче</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀кохте</td>
></tr>

><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">влѐче</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">вля̀коха</td>
></tr>
></table>

>

The second and third subclasses of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contain the [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) which satisfy the following conditions simultaneously:

- The [present stem](#Present%20Tense) has an etymologycally non-[palatalized](TODO), non-[fricative](TODO) consonant before the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ and this consonant does not undergo alternations when forming the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms).
- The [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is derived from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) by replacing the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ with the aorist thematic vowel ⟨а⟩.

A few things to note:

- [Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a [present stem](#Present%20Forms) ending in *-ерѐ* drop the ⟨е⟩ before the ⟨р⟩ in the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms).
- The [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) remains in the same place as the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).
- [Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) without a prefix as well as all [imperfective](#Aspect) [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a prefix are allowed to shift the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) to the aorist thematic vowel. Both variations are equivalent but the latter forms are slowly being phased out.

>[!INFO] Info: Subclass 2 vs Subclass 3
>
>The sole difference between subclasses 2 and 3 lies in the fact that [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) in subclass 3 have a dictionary form ending in *-я*: *капя*, *къпя*, *хапя*, etc.
>

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclasses 2 and 3 of Conjugation Class I
>

><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Since the verb does not have prefixes, the stress of the aorist stem can fall on the same place as the stress of the present stem or it can shift to the aorist thematic vowel. ><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Since the verb does not have prefixes, the stress of the aorist stem can fall on the same place as the stress of the present stem or it can shift to the aorist thematic vowel.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>

>

The fourth subclass of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contains the [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) which satisfy the following conditions simultaneously:

- The [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is derived from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) by replacing the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ with the aorist thematic vowel ⟨а⟩.
- The [present stem](#Present%20Tense) has an etymologycally non-[palatalized](TODO), non-[fricative](TODO) consonant before the thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ which undergoes the following alternations when forming the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms): ⟨ж⟩ ~ ⟨з⟩, ⟨ж⟩ ~ ⟨г⟩, ⟨ч⟩ ~ ⟨к⟩ and ⟨ш⟩ ~ ⟨с⟩.

A few things to note:

- The [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) remains in the same place as the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).
- [Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) without a prefix as well as all [imperfective](#Aspect) [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a prefix are allowed to shift the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) to the aorist thematic vowel. Both variations are equivalent but the latter forms are slowly being phased out.

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclass 4 of Conjugation Class I

>

The fifth subclass of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contains the [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) which satisfy the following conditions simultaneously:

- The [present](#Present%20Tense) first-person singular ends in *-а*.
- The [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is derived from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) by replacing the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ with the aorist thematic vowel ⟨а⟩ combined with a historical palatalization of the preceding constonant, ultimately resulting in a thematic ⟨я⟩.

This subclass contains only a handful of simple [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) and their derivatives.

The [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) of these [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) is always [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the thematic ⟨я⟩.

The sixth subclass of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contains the [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) which satisfy the following conditions simultaneously:

- The [present](#Present%20Tense) first-person singular ends in ⟨а⟩, ⟨ъ⟩, ⟨о⟩, ⟨у⟩, ⟨е⟩, ⟨и⟩, ⟨ю⟩ or ⟨я⟩ followed by *-я*.
- The [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is derived from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) by replacing the present thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ with a thematic ⟨я⟩ after non-[fricative](TODO) consonants and into ⟨а⟩ after [fricative](TODO) consonants.

A few things to note:

- The [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) remains in the same place as the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).
- [Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) without a prefix as well as all [imperfective](#Aspect) [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a prefix are allowed to shift the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) to the aorist thematic vowel. Both variations are equivalent but the latter forms are slowly being phased out.
- Many of these also have [aorist forms](#Aorist%20Forms) according to subclass 7.

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclass 6 of Conjugation Class I
>
><table>
><caption>ба̀я, ба̀еш, ба̀я̀х, ба̀я̀ ('to chant incantations', imperfective)</caption>
><tr>

><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Since the verb does not have prefixes, the stress of the aorist stem can fall on the same place as the stress of the present stem or it can shift to the aorist thematic vowel.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀я, бая̀</td>

></tr>

></tr>

>

The seventh subclass of [conjugation I](#Present%20Tense) contains [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) characterized by the following:

- The [present](#Present%20Forms) first-person singular ends in *-а̀я*, *-ъ̀я*, *-о̀я*, *-у̀я*, *-ѝя*, *-ю̀я* or *-я̀я* and the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is formed by removing the thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms). 
- The [present](#Present%20Forms) first-person singular ends in *-éя* and the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is formed by removing the thematic vowel ⟨е⟩ from the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) and changing the preceding [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) ⟨е⟩ into a [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) ⟨я⟩.

The [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) remains in the same place as the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclass 7 of Conjugation Class I

### Second-Conjugation Aorist Forms

The first subclass of [conjugation II](#Present%20Forms) contains the [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) whose [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is identical to the [present stem](#Present%20Forms). 

- If the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) is [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the present thematic vowel ⟨и⟩, then the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) is also [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the aorist thematic vowel ⟨и⟩.
- For [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) without prefixes as well as [imperfective](#Aspect) [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with prefixes, if the [present stem](#Present%20Forms) is [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the syllable before the present thematic vowel ⟨и⟩, then the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) can be [stressed](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) on the same syllable or on the aorist thematic vowel ⟨и⟩. The latter forms are becoming increasingly rare.

A few things to note:

- The [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) remains in the same place as the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [present stem](#Present%20Forms).
- [Verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) without a prefix as well as all [imperfective](#Aspect) [verbs](./Verbs%20(Bulgarian).md) with a prefix are allowed to shift the [stress](./Phonology%20(Bulgarian).md#Stress) of the [aorist stem](#Aorist%20Forms) to the aorist thematic vowel. Both variations are equivalent but the latter forms are slowly being phased out.

>[!EXAMPLE]- Example: Subclass 1 of Conjugation II
>
><table>
><caption>ба̀вя, ба̀виш, ба̀вѝх, ба̀вѝ ('to slow down', imperfective)</caption>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle"></th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Singular</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Plural</th>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Notes</th>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">First Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀вих, бавѝх</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀вихме, бавѝхме</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle" rowspan=3>Since the verb does not have prefixes, the stress of the aorist stem can fall on the same place as the stress of the present stem or it can shift to the aorist thematic vowel.</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Second Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀ви, бавѝ</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀вихте, бавѝхте</td>
></tr>
><tr>
><th style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">Third Person</th>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀ви, бавѝ</td>
><td style="text-align:center;vertical-align:middle">ба̀виха, бавѝха</td>
></tr>
></table>
>

### Third-Conjugation Aorist Forms
